## Calibration subset and matched draw on the literature (1−b) masses (2026-10-09, author)

- **Planck (1−b) = 0.78**, the baseline the Planck 2015 cosmology paper adopts for the PSZ2
  hydrostatic MSZ ("We adopt the CCCP mass calibration as our baseline", Planck 2015 XXIV §4.3,
  Table 2); the PSZ2 catalogue paper itself defines MSZ as the hydrostatic mass of the assumed
  scaling relation and applies no (1−b). ACT DR6 M500c (already lensing-calibrated) and the
  lensing masses: no correction. WtG: Applegate+14's ~1.2 % simulation calibration not applied
  (negligible against the mass errors).
- **Calibration subset**: the K=5 replica (`campaigns/v4`) fails gate P on the corrected masses
  (mass KS D 0.142 vs 0.061); it stays the donor-assignment base of `campaigns/v4_max`, and the
  calibration set becomes a K'=5, R=9, dz_max 0.25 matched draw from the maximal list
  (`campaigns/v4_max/calibration_k5_*`: 3,157 draws, gate P passes).
- **Matched draw**: K'=10, R=12, dz_max 0.25, TNG-Cluster + TNG300
  (`campaigns/v4_max/sampler_recommended_*`: 6,289 draws, gate P passes strictly).
  (Superseded 2026-10-12: on the 9-snapshot list the main draw is K'=5, R=12, dz_max 0.25
  (3,111 draws, gate P strict) and the calibration subset K'=5, R=9; `campaigns/v4_max/README.md`.)

## Headline intrinsic hydrostatic mass: Barnes et al. 2021 with fitted slopes (2026-10-12, author)

> Superseded later the same day: the headline is `barnes21_nosf_fit_mu` (each cell's own mu), and
> `barnes21_nosf_fit` is not emitted ("Intrinsic hydrostatic truth: one methodological line").

- **v4 and the box presets: `sim.hse_pressure = 'thermal-fit'`**, so `m500c_hse_msun` is
  `barnes21_nosf_fit`: Vikhlinin fits of the true 3D gas density and mass-weighted
  temperature with analytic slopes, as Barnes et al. 2021 do and as X-ray hydrostatic analyses
  do (smooth parametric profiles differentiated analytically, not finite differences between
  shells). This is the intrinsic hydrostatic mass (the "red line") the MBProj2D fit is
  compared with; the true total mass is expected to lie above it (non-thermal pressure,
  mergers). The finite-difference `barnes21_nosf` stays as its own column.
- **Star-forming gas excluded**, as before: TNG gives it the effective equation-of-state
  temperature of the star-formation model, not a physical gas temperature.
- **New `hse_crossing` value `fit-failed`**: a fitted headline whose Vikhlinin fits did not
  converge has a NaN `m500c_hse_msun` with this flag (before, it would have read `none`, "no
  crossing"); C12 accepts NaN for `none` and `fit-failed` only.
- The v4 spec hashes change (the regeneration with photons from 0.2 keV changes them anyway).

## SAS-ready products: photons from 0.2 keV, PPS band 1, SAS headers, SAS detection (2026-10-12)

Aim: ESA's standard `edetect_chain` (SAS 22.1, the five PPS bands, three cameras jointly) runs
identically on the v4 mocks and on real XMM data.  Everything below is behind three new
instrument spec fields and the photon floor, switched on for `v4` and the box presets only
(`v32`, `v32_lowz`: byte-identical products, `sim_exact`, `instrument_exact`,
`photometry_exact`, `campaign_fixture` pass).  Spec hashes (135 hashed fields): v32
`5e99f10979271968`, v32_lowz `55d54cc7c3ebabc1`, v4 `0ee2ed3024bec5d1`, v4_tng300
`c3638bdb294186c9`, v4_tng100 `b63c7159e875f3f1`, v4_tng50 `318a1cb4e052b551` (were
`119c873cd72c30ea`, `a62eebcef1cef992`, `95df5d7494a5fb23`, `aa7f1ac74b477f4e`,
`68c0be19015aba0d`, `1119f395e89c8282`; the legacy hashes move with the new fields, their
products do not).

- **Photon floor 0.2 keV** (`sim.emin_phot_kev`, v4 and box presets).  The floor is in the REST
  frame (observed energy = E/(1+z)), so 0.2 keV guarantees every photon with an observed energy
  of at least 0.2 keV exists; the old 0.3 keV left observed 0.2-0.29 keV empty below z = 0.5
  (z = 0.05: a third of band 1).  Places that assumed 0.3 keV: the spec default, `SimOptions`,
  `generate_photons` (all legacy defaults), `instrument/compute_ecf.py` (the ECF table is built on
  0.3-10 keV rest spectra but is only used for 0.5-2 keV observed bands, which need rest energies
  of at least 0.5 keV: unchanged); `get_apec_var_spec` accepts 0.2 (energy grid 0.2-20 keV in 5000
  bins), the TBabs table (`apply_absorption`) runs from 0.01 keV.  The instrument stage already
  simulated AGN and both backgrounds down to 0.2 keV: the ARF and the sky-background spectrum
  are on a grid from 0.1 keV, the RMF Monte-Carlo energies start at 0.05 keV (pn) / 0 (MOS), the
  FWC particle spectrum covers every channel; checked in `tests/e2e/test_instrument_sas_ready.py`
  (band 1 events exist).  No change to the instrument was needed for the floor.
- **Band 1 products.**  `instrument.product_bands = 'pps-1-5'`: the stage writes the PPS bands
  1000-5000 (0.2-0.5, 0.5-1, 1-2, 2-4.5, 4.5-12 keV) for every camera, the PPS names
  `P<obsid>{PNS003,M1S001,M2S002}{IMAGE_,EXPMAP}1000.FTZ`: 30 files per view.  The merged image
  and the fitter's bands are unchanged (0.5-2 keV; the fits read 2000-5000).  The EXPMAP of
  band 1 is vignetted at the count-weighted band energy, as the others.  Campaign check C2 expects
  the five bands (and the SAS keywords) under these switches.
- **pn band 1: single-pixel events.**  The PPS pn image of band 1 is selected with
  `PATTERN == 0 && RAWY > 12` (taken from the DSS keywords of the donor PPS image
  `P0551830101PNS003IMAGE_1000.FTZ`: `DSVAL3 = 0`, `DSVAL2 = (12:`), the other bands with
  `PATTERN <= 4`.  The mocks carry no event patterns.  Decision: the CCF holds the pattern
  fractions, so no model of our own: `EPN_QUANTUMEF_0018.CCF`, extension `FRACTION_ENERGY`
  (columns `FRAC_S`, `FRAC_D`, `FRAC_T`, `FRAC_Q`, `FRAC_SD`, `FRAC_SDTQ` on `EBINS_FRACTION`;
  the table `apply_pattern_loss` already used for the PATTERN <= 4 efficiency; Users' Handbook
  section 3.3 only describes the patterns).  The events of the stage already stand for
  PATTERN <= 4 (the ARF carries FRAC_SD/FRAC_SDTQ under `pattern_loss_factor != 1`, v4), so each
  band-1 event is kept with probability FRAC_S/FRAC_SD at its channel energy (full frame: 1.00
  at 0.2 keV, 0.99 at 0.25, 0.96 at 0.3, 0.89 at 0.4, 0.84 at 0.5 keV; the 4 modes differ by
  < 0.02), mean 0.93-0.96 over a soft spectrum (header `SINGLEFR`).  The EXPMAP is NOT scaled:
  SAS `eexpmap` documents that it evaluates only TIME, GTI and CCD-number DSS filters ("DSS
  filtering of other event properties (spatial, pattern, energy ...) still needs to be
  implemented") and that its exposure map has QE = 1, i.e. normalised to the all-patterns case;
  the 3XMM ECFs of pn band 1 take the pattern selection into account.
- **pn band 1: particle background and rows at the node.**  The simulator's QPB spectrum is the
  FWC event rate with `FLAG == 0` and ALL patterns; in 0.2-0.5 keV the pn FWC events are mostly
  doubles (electronic noise: 1.32 PATTERN <= 4 events/s at 0.2-0.3 keV against 0.13 singles).  The
  unmodified QPB made the mock pn band 1 2.5 times brighter than band 2 in total counts (fixture
  view; real donor 0884710101, outskirts: band 1/band 2 = 1.2) and was the reason the SAS thread's PATTERN <= 4 band 1 adds
  tens of spurious point sources per field.  The band-1 channels of the QPB are scaled so that
  the net retention equals the FWC single-pixel fraction (`pn_qpb_single_table`: `FLAG == 0 &&
  PATTERN == 0 && RAWY > 12` over `FLAG == 0`, per 20 eV; mean 0.10 at 0.2-0.3 keV, 0.25 at
  0.3-0.4, 0.49 at 0.4-0.5 keV): pn band-1 rate in the outskirts mock / real PPS = 0.95 (band 2: 0.97).  Events
  within 12 rows of the readout node (RAWY <= 12; the node is at the outer end of each CCD
  column: in the real clean event list RAWY 13-40 sit at 14 000 DET units from the centre,
  RAWY 190-200 at 4 900) are dropped in band 1.  Finding: the real cleaned pn event lists
  contain NO event with RAWY <= 12 in ANY band (`#XMMEA_EP` removes them), and the PPS EXPMAPs
  still expose those rows (eexpmap, above); the mocks keep 3.6-6.9 % of their pn events there in
  bands 2-5 (5.7 % band 1).  Open point (not changed, outside the request): apply the RAWY cut to
  every pn band.
- **PA_PNT.**  `PA_PNT = (POSANGLE + 114.2157) mod 360` (SAS convention: position angle of the
  detector, north through east).  The prototype's estimate `(86 - POSANGLE) mod 360` is the wrong
  sense.  Resolved on the five donors of the prototype and the two real observations of the
  fixtures: the exposure-map footprint of the instrument stage rolled by `PA_PNT - PA_REF`
  (`PA_REF = 114.2157`, the `PA_PNT` of obsid 0827340901 whose masks the stage rolls; the
  roll is counter-clockwise on the north-up, east-left image, i.e. increasing position angle)
  correlates 0.78-0.93 (mean of pn, MOS1, MOS2) with the donor's PPS EXPMAP2000, against
  0.68-0.84 for the mirrored `PA_REF - PA_PNT`, 0.77-0.90 for the 180 degree flip and 0.70-0.86
  for the prototype's rule (per donor in `tests/e2e/test_instrument_sas_ready.py`, which runs the
  same comparison on the fixtures).  The pn footprint alone cannot tell the 180 degree flip (0.92
  against 0.91); the MOS footprints can (0.93 against 0.87).  The repository already used this
  relation (`donors/build_core_offsets.py`: a mock with POSANGLE P is a real observation with
  `PA_PNT = P + PA_REF`).  `emldetect` uses PA_PNT to orient the ellbeta PSF.
- **SAS header keywords** (`instrument/sas_headers.py`, `instrument.sas_headers = 'on'`):
  `TELESCOP`, `OBS_MODE`, `DATAMODE = IMAGING`; `OBS_ID`, `EXP_ID` (numeric string
  `<obsid><nnn>` with the exposure number of the donor's PPS exposure map), `EXPIDSTR`,
  `DATE-OBS` of the donor (table `donor_active_ccds.csv`; `MOCK = T` and `DONORID` stay; the
  identifiers are the donor's because `cifbuild` selects the CCF issue, and with it the PSF, by
  the observation date); `RA_PNT`, `DEC_PNT` (WCS of the optical-axis pixel); `ONTIMEnn` per
  imaged CCD (nn = CCD number, as the PPS headers: MOS1 with CCD 3 and 6 lost has ONTIME01, 02,
  04, 05, 07), equal to the camera live time; pn `FRMTIME` and `TIMEDEL` = frame time x
  `OOTFRAC` of the mode (73.4 ms x 0.936 = 0.0687 s, 199.1 ms x 0.977 = 0.1945 s: the PPS values
  to 1e-3); the PPS physical sky-coordinate keywords (`REFX/REFY*`, `CTYPEnL`, `LTVn`, ...) with
  the physical pixel 25921 on the optical axis.  The TAN WCS (cluster at CRPIX, CRVAL = 150, +2)
  is NOT touched: the fitter reads it.  Every integer keyword beyond 32 bits is a string
  (`VIEWSEED`; SAS reads FITS headers through the DAL, which cannot parse 64-bit integers); read
  back with the FITS reader in the tests, and campaign check C2 refuses a wide integer.
- **Adapter** (`instrument/sas_detect.py`, `tngxmm instrument sas-detect`, Kedro node
  `instrument.run_sas_detection`).  One call for mocks and real observations, the prototype's
  settings (METHOD.md of the SAS prototype): images and exposure maps staged as
  `<cam>_image_b<k>[exp].fits`, `cifbuild` for the DATE-OBS of the headers, one `edetect_chain`
  with all three cameras and bands 1-5 (`witheexpmap=no`, `esp_nsplinenodes=16`, `esen_mlmin=15`,
  3XMM-DR6 ECFs by camera and filter, other parameters at the task defaults,
  `OMP_NUM_THREADS=1`, `taskset` on the login node).  A real observation is run on its PPS
  products (longest exposure per camera, `witheexpmap=no` on the PPS exposure maps), not through
  `eexpmap` as the prototype's ODF route: the same settings and the same exposure-map source as
  the mocks.  Output next to the products: `sas_detect/emllist.fits`, `sources.csv`, the SAS log,
  `provenance.json`.

### Off-axis PSF of the mocks: assessment (report only, nothing implemented)

> Superseded 2026-11-03 ("Independent review of the replacements, round 1", item C): the cluster photons get the
> CCF King PSF interpolated in energy and off-axis angle (`instrument.psf_model = 'king-energy-angle'`, the default
> in `src/tngxmm/config.py`); the fit now uses the King at each pixel's off-axis angle (`massfit.psf = 'king-angle'`).

The prototype found 1.3-2.4 times too many mock point detections beyond 10' off-axis: the mocks
use ONE round King profile (row 0 of `KING_PARAMS`: 225 eV, on axis; pn r0 = 5.495", alpha =
1.588) at every energy and off-axis angle (`king_offsets`; upstream `xmm_simulator.imaging.
psf_convole(_evt)` does the same, its comment: "Sticking to on-axis, outside doesn't matter so
much"), while `emldetect` fits the CCF `ellbeta` model (default since SAS 5.17.2).

- **What the CCF holds** (`XRT1/2/3_XPSF`, issues 16/16/18): `KING_PARAMS` (80 rows: 10 energies
  0.225-13.25 keV x 8 angles 0-14', r0 and alpha), `ELLBETA_PARAMS` (56 rows: 8 energies 0.1-15
  keV x 7 angles 0-15', PARAMS = r0 [arcsec], beta, ellipticity; header keys OPHI = 11.25, NSP =
  16, RTHRESH, CHNGFRAC, M1FRAC, M2FRAC, SECFRAC, U, V, RD1-3, OFF_0-3 describe the azimuthal
  and secondary structure, which the CCF does not document), 512 x 512 PSF images (10 energies x
  6 angles 0-15'), single and triple Gaussian parameters, slew PSF.  pn, 1.5 keV: ellipticity
  0.006 (0'), 0.16 (6'), 0.31 (9'), 0.48 (12'), 0.62 (15'); r0 5.6" -> 8.4" (12') -> 11.5"
  (15'); the round-equivalent radius of 87 % of the flux 31" -> 49" (12') -> 76" (15').  The
  KING rows alone change little with angle at 1.5-2.75 keV (R50 8.2-8.4") and by 37 % at 8 keV (XRT3
  12'); the elongation is what the King model lacks.
- **What it would take.**  (a) Energy and angle dependent King: the sampler takes arrays; cluster
  photons have E and the camera angle before the PSF, the AGN need their true energy drawn before
  the RMF (now they draw RMF channels directly: a restructuring of `agn_channel_cdfs` and a new
  RNG order, switch-gated).  About 1-2 days with tests (estimate; the stage time is dominated by the backgrounds, 20-30 s per view).  Cheapest and
  fixes the width but not the ellipticity.  (b) The CCF PSF images as sampling kernels (inverse
  CDF per (E, angle) bin, rotated to the azimuth of the source about the optical axis): exact
  shapes, no reverse engineering of `ellbeta`; the 60 images per camera are 63 MB of CDFs, the
  telescope-to-detector axis convention and the image pixel scale (keyword Y_PIXSZ = 0.0363,
  units to be confirmed) must be checked against `emldetect` on injected point sources.  About 3 days.  (c) `ellbeta` itself:
  needs the Read et al. / Traulsen et al. definition of the azimuthal modulation (not in the
  CCF); 4-5 days with the validation.  Any of them is gated by a new `instrument.psf_model`
  (`king-onaxis` default and header `PSFMODEL`); the legacy and the current v4 products stay.
  Validation for all three: inject point sources at 0-15' in a flat field, run `emldetect`
  (`tngxmm instrument sas-detect`) and compare DET_ML, extent and position with the on-axis
  expectation; the off-axis excess of the unmatched detections must vanish.
- **Effect on the fitter.**  The fit models the on-axis King of the camera (`massfit/fit_psf.py`:
  for mocks the first row of the header's `PSFFILE`, "exactly what the instrument stage draws its
  photon offsets from"; for real data the on-axis rows interpolated in energy; "the off-axis
  broadening is not modelled"), one kernel per image.  Today the mock fit is therefore
  PSF-exact and isolates the fitter; an off-axis mock would put the real-data PSF mismatch into
  the mocks.  Size: the cluster signal inside 5' sees a PSF within 5 % of the on-axis one, so
  the profile is untouched there; the effect sits where the surface brightness is low and
  point sources matter: the point-source mask radius rule (`lx_mask_*`, `massfit` source masks;
  King EEF 0.87 radius, 31") would leave 21 % of an AGN's flux unmasked at 12' (the 12' PSF has
  79 % inside 31") against 13 %, 1.6 times more AGN wing flux in the background annulus and
  the outer bins, and the point-source detection threshold of the mask list (per-donor
  detection limit) would be set with an off-axis PSF the mask does not know.  Recommended
  order: first run the fits on the (a) mocks (cheap) to see whether the outskirts and Lx
  backgrounds move, and make the mask radius depend on the angle; do (b)/(c) only if the SAS
  detection of the mocks is to be compared with the PPS lists beyond 10'.

### SAS-ready products: validation and cost (login node, 2026-10-12)

- **Tests.**  Legacy byte-exact end-to-end (`sim_exact`, `instrument_exact`, `photometry_exact`,
  `campaign_fixture`): 69 passed.  V4 end-to-end (`campaign_v4_replica`, `campaign_v4_tng300`,
  `instrument_{windows,active_ccds,oot,switches,placement}`): 35 passed (the full v4 preset
  test now expects 30 files).  New: `tests/unit/test_instrument_sas_ready.py` (25),
  `test_instrument_sas_detect.py` (11), `tests/e2e/test_instrument_sas_ready.py` (7: 30 products,
  keywords, no integer keyword beyond 32 bits in any header the stage writes, read back with the
  FITS reader, pn band 1 against the all-patterns replay, FWC single-pixel rates, PA_PNT against the
  PPS exposure maps of the two fixture observations).  Unit suite (`make test`): 911 passed, 39 skipped (no mbproj2d in this environment).
  `campaign check` on the nine regenerated views (TNG-Cluster snapshot 45, three halos x three
  views): C1-C4, C6, C8, C10-C15 pass, including C2 with the five bands, the SAS keywords and the
  32-bit rule and C14 (band 1: 0.02 % of the counts on EXPMAP == 0 in every camera); C16/C17 fail
  only because the check ran on a tree written by `campaign run` without the run stamp and
  without the `truth-sphere` stage.
- **Cost** (one worker; 9 views at z = 1.2, 3 views at z = 0.05-0.075 of TNG-Cluster, FoF loader;
  `old` = v4 before this change).  Per view: photon generation + absorption 1.5 s -> 1.5 s
  (z = 1.2), 8.5 s -> 9.3 s (z = 0.06, +9 %); photons 13 421 -> 13 427 (+0.05 %) at z = 1.2,
  3.79 M -> 4.32 M (+13.8 %) at z = 0.06; instrument stage 21.9 s (old) / 22.9 s (floor 0.3
  keV, five bands) / 21.5 s (new) at z = 1.2, 27.2 s -> 27.5 s at z = 0.06; the whole view 46 s
  at z = 1.2 (412 s for nine views, old 414 s) and 98 s at z = 0.06 (294 s for three, floor 0.3
  keV 286 s).  Disk: percam 2.74 -> 3.45 MB per view (+26 %), `events_v*_abs.h5` +14 % at low z.
  The floor adds 4-8 % to the pn band-1 counts (3-5 % MOS) and 0.2-2 % to band 2 at z = 0.056-
  0.075; nothing at z = 1.2 (rest 0.3 keV is observed 0.14 keV).  One-off: the pn FWC single-
  pixel table reads 14.4 M events once per process (1.1 s, peak 0.7 GB of the process with the
  imports; the columns could be read in chunks if a node budget needs it).  SAS itself is the
  new cost: `edetect_chain` 5 bands, one core: 110-150 s (18-30 sources), 410 s (110), 1630 s
  (220), 2490 s (250, 17 extended); 4 bands 1.1-2.6 times less.
- **Band 1 against real data** (outskirts 8-13', count rate per band, mock/real PPS of the same
  donor 0884710101, view h7122163 v2): pn band 1 0.95, band 2 0.97, band 3 0.78, band 4 0.75
  (the real field holds a cluster); MOS2 band 1/2 1.08/0.98 (MOS1: the real field's MOS1 is 2.7
  times brighter than its MOS2 in these bands: an observation-specific excess, not modelled).
  The unmodified QPB gave a pn band 1 2.5 times its band 2 (fixture view).
- **SAS `edetect_chain`, 5 bands, three of the regenerated views plus one TNG300 view** (all
  detections `DET_ML > 10`, 6" matching radius, injected AGN inside the pn footprint; same halos
  and donors as the prototype's mocks, other projections and AGN draws; the 4-band rows are the
  same views with bands 2-5, i.e. the prototype's configuration):

  | view (donor) | bands | N_pt | N_ext | N_AGN | unmatched (orphans > 30") | C(2-5e-15) | C(5-10e-15) | C(1-2e-14) | C(> 2e-14) | F_det/F_X |
  |---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

  Prototype 4-band mocks (SASDET, same halos and donors): N_pt 216 / 91 / 214 / 30, completeness
  (2-5e-15) 0.62 / 0.15 / 0.59 / 0.00, (5-10e-15) 0.90 / 0.46 / 0.81 / 0.05, (1-2e-14) 0.97 /
  0.82 / 1.00 / 0.30, (> 2e-14) 0.96 / 1.00 / 1.00 / 0.94, F_det/F_X 1.72 / 1.69 / 1.77 / 1.86,
  unmatched 4 / 4 / 4 / 1: our 4-band rows on the regenerated views reproduce them (same
  donors).  Band 1 does not change the point-source completeness (differences within the
  Poisson noise of 15-60 sources per bin), raises F_det/F_X by 0.1-0.2 (the EPIC flux of
  `emldetect` covers 0.2-12 keV, the injected F_X is the hard-band flux) and, for the two fields of
  donor 0884710101 (MOS1 with 5 CCDs), adds 7 and 12 EXTENDED detections (5 and 11 more
  unmatched ones, of which 3 and 5 more than 30" from any injected AGN).  They are MOS1-only,
  band-1-only sources (MOS1 band-1 DET_ML 23-139, pn and MOS2 about 0, bands 2-5 about 0) at
  9-14' off-axis with extents of 6-16"; in the four examples inspected the fitted position has no
  MOS1 exposure in the products (SAS reports a MOS1 exposure there, MASKFRAC 0.88).  The
  mock band-1 images show no edge excess in the count rate (rim/interior 1.0, real MOS1 1.8)
  and 0.02 % of the counts on zero exposure.  The real 5-band run of the same donor has 13
  extended sources (11 in 4 bands), so the mock with band 1 is close to the real count of extended
  sources.  Cause not found; open point.
- **Real observation through the adapter** (0747420131, PPS products, one pn/MOS1/MOS2 exposure
  each): 17 point + 1 extended sources against 17 point sources in the PPS list, 16 of the 17
  recovered, median flux ratio 1.20, DET_ML ratio 1.04, median position offset 0.47"
  (`compare.py real` of the prototype on the adapter's list); the prototype's ODF route
  recovered 76-82 % of this observation's list.
- **Off-axis excess.**  The unmatched POINT detections per field are 0-2 in every off-axis bin
  (0-5', 5-10', 10-15') in both configurations (the table `summarize_new` of the validation
  prints them), i.e. no visible off-axis excess of false point sources in these four fields.
  The prototype's 1.3-2.4 times excess beyond 10' was measured as a density of detections
  against the expectation and is not reproduced here.  The unmatched detections that are not
  band-1-only (h7122163 v2: 3, besides the cluster) lie 9-12" from an injected AGN of 3e-15 to
  1e-14 erg/s/cm2 and were fitted with 3-4" extents at 6-13' off-axis: consistent with the
  PSF mismatch assessed below, not proof of it.

### SAS-ready products: open points

> Since 2026-10-26 ("Release 1.1.2 (2026-10-26): the code issues of the docstring audit"): open point 6 is fixed,
> `schemas.products.PERCAM_RE` accepts the 13-character obsid.

1. **RAWY <= 12 in every pn band.** (RESOLVED 2026-10-12: `instrument.pn_edge_rows = 'drop'`, EXPMAP
   unchanged; see "pn readout-edge rows in every band".)  Real cleaned pn event lists have no event at RAWY <= 12 in any
   band; the mocks drop them in band 1 only (3.6-6.9 % of the pn events of bands 2-5 sit there).
   Applying the cut to every pn band (EXPMAP unchanged, as eexpmap) would make the pn mocks
   PPS-like in all bands; it changes the v4 fit inputs at the outermost 49" of the pn CCDs, so it
   is left to the author.
2. **MOS1 band-1 extended detections** (above): +7 / +12 extended sources in the two deep fields of
   the 5-CCD MOS1 donor; cause not found; real data of the same donor show the same number.
3. **Off-axis PSF** (above): report only.
4. **Donor identities in the headers.**  `OBS_ID`, `EXP_ID`, `DATE-OBS` are the donor's (with
   `MOCK = T`, `DONORID`); a mock is never to be offered as the donor's data.
5. **CIF.**  (RESOLVED 2026-10-12: the analysis date is fixed, `CCF_ANALYSIS_DATE`.)  `cifbuild`
   used the current Valid CCF set and the analysis date of the run; for a
   reproducible source list pass `DetectSettings.analysisdate`.
6. **Product name schema.**  `schemas.products.PERCAM_RE` still accepts the v1.0 mock obsid
   (`\d{9}v\d`), not the fixed-width v4 form (`067` + 8 digits + `v0`); the campaign check uses its
   own pattern.  Not touched here.
7. **Memory.**  The FWC single-pixel table reads three columns of 14.4 M events once per
   process (about 0.3 GB transient).

## Full snapshots only; mini-snapshot fallback; composition-aware truth (2026-10-12, author)

- **Production uses TNG FULL snapshots only (reverses "Mini snapshots kept, flagged").**
  Author's reason: full metallicity information (`GFM_Metals`, `GFM_CoolingRate` exist only on
  full snapshots; the mini-snapshot fallbacks cost +2.9 % in `lx_intr_r500`). `sim.snaps` of
  `v4` and, through `_v4_box`, `v4_tng300`, `v4_tng100`, `v4_tng50`: 99, 91, 84, 78, 72, 67,
  **59**, 50, 40 (was 17 snapshots, 9 mini; snapshot 59, z = 0.70, bridges the 0.5-1.0 gap
  between 67 and 50). The full schedule is `snapshots.FULL_SNAPSHOTS` (0 and 1 also exist but
  carry no GFM fields; the module's 20-member set excludes them). `v32`, `v32_lowz` unchanged
  (v32 keeps its mini snapshots: byte-exact regressions). Spec hashes (old -> new, 16 hex):
  v4 `b9819d0c106d5fcb` -> `8740cd068aff7cff`, v4_tng300 `1f16a68f23805cc0` ->
  `595d923291dba71f`, v4_tng100 `bfa772b0412862d2` -> `97b5f21dd43053ea`, v4_tng50
  `fbfa2c284d4a52de` -> `1783a94141a33b46` (full digests: INTERFACES section 5; v32 and
  v32_lowz unchanged). The new truth column and the metallicity profiles below do not change
  any spec hash (`TRUTH_COLUMNS` and the truth-profile file grow additively).
- **Target lists rebuilt on the 9 snapshots** with the existing builders (commands in
  `campaigns/{v4,v4_max,v4_box}/README.md`, same seeds, K = 5, cap 3, literature (1-b)
  matching masses). Halo table: the old one restricted to the snapshots plus snapshot 59 from
  the TNG-Cluster group catalogue (252 primary targets with M500c >= 1e14); TNG300 / TNG100 /
  TNG50 tables regenerated by `sim box-halos`. Result (before -> after): replica 3,092 ->
  2,840 views on 1,380 -> 1,082 halo-snapshots, coverage 98.4 % -> 93.4 %, **gate P FAILS**
  (mass D 0.142 -> 0.241, critical 0.061; coverage below 95 %), 46 -> 432 epoch-fallback views,
  |z_obs - z_snap| median 0.015 -> 0.033, p90 0.042 -> 0.088; v4_max 18,549 -> 9,615 views; v4_box
  297 -> 159 views; the K' = 5 calibration subset still passes strictly, the K' = 10 draw only
  through p_neff. 14 ACT observations (z = 1.16-1.34) have no snapshot within the +-0.15 epoch
  window (snapshots 50 and 40 are 0.5 apart); no variant of K or of the window passes the
  mass gate. NOT changed: the epoch rule, K, the reuse cap; proposals in `campaigns/v4/README.md`
  (`epoch_candidates = 3`, `epoch_max_dz = 0.25` restore the coverage to 0.972) await the author.
- **Gas loader without `GFM_Metals`** (`load_halo_gas.load_halo_gas`): the documented
  intent "solar ratios per cell" is now implemented: `Z_elements = GFM_Metallicity / Z_sun`
  of the abundance table (`compute_apec_spectra.solar_metal_mass_fraction`: elements Z >= 3
  over the sum of H..Zn of the soxs table: `angr` 0.01937, `lodd` 0.01321) for all seven
  elements; before it was 1 x solar for every cell regardless of its metallicity. The full-snapshot
  path is untouched (unit test, the bit-exact V3.2 regressions that run on the login node).
  v4 no longer reaches this path in production (full snapshots only); it stays for v32 and
  for any mini snapshot a user requests.
- **New truth column `barnes21_nosf_fit_mu` (composition-aware intrinsic HSE mass).** The same
  50 linear shells, non-star-forming gas, filling density, Vikhlinin fits and analytic slopes as
  `barnes21_nosf_fit`, but the shell mass-weighted `<T/mu>` with each cell's own mu
  (`mu = 4 / (1 + 3 X_H + 4 X_H x_e)`, X_H of the cell from `GFM_Metals`, 0.76 where absent)
  replaces T and the fixed mu = 0.59: `M = -(k r / (G m_p)) <T/mu> (dln rho/dln r + dln <T/mu>/dln r)`,
  the Vikhlinin T form fitted to `<T/mu>` (`_m_fit(..., mu=1)`). Columns
  `m500_hse_`, `mhse_..._r500c_over_m500c`, `mu_hse_` (NaN: no single mu) and
  `hse_fit_ok_barnes21_nosf_fit_mu`; selectable as the headline with
  `hse_pressure = 'thermal-fit-mu'` (set as the headline further down; `barnes21_nosf_fit` is not
  emitted any more, see "Intrinsic hydrostatic truth: one methodological line"). Note for the
  author: because `T = (gamma-1) u mu m_p / k`, the cell's mu cancels in `T/mu`: the quantity is the
  internal energy per unit mass, so the column equals the pressure-based thermal HSE mass with
  fitted slopes, and differs from `barnes21_nosf_fit` only through the departure of the cells'
  mu from 0.59 (and through the T weighting of mu inside a shell): per-cell X_H is read but cannot
  change the number. The metallicity of the gas enters only through X_H of the weights below.
- **Emission-weighted metallicity profiles** (truth profile, additive, format stays 3):
  `metallicity/{nonsf,mock}/{Z_ew,Z_fe_ew}` on the 60-shell grid, weights `n_e n_H V` with the
  cell's X_H, `Z_ew = GFM_Metallicity / Z_sun`, `Z_fe_ew = X_Fe / X_Fe,sun` (GFM_Metals column 8)
  in solar units of the fit's abundance table (`sim.apec_abund_table`, `lodd` in v4; root
  attributes `metallicity_abund_table`, `metallicity_z_sun`, `metallicity_xfe_sun`); summary
  scalars `z_ew_r500c_{nonsf,mock}`, `z_fe_ew_r500c_{nonsf,mock}` in `summary_json` (not view-table
  columns). The truth gas read gained `GFM_Metals` (optional) and `GFM_Metallicity`; the
  spherical reader returns `GFM_Metals` as its H and Fe columns (N, 2) (was H only); a source
  without `GFM_Metallicity` still works (NaN).
- **Headline (author, final 2026-10-12): `barnes21_nosf_fit_mu` is the v4 headline** (v4 and the
  box presets: `hse_pressure = 'thermal-fit-mu'`); `barnes21_nosf_fit` (mu = 0.59) stayed a column
  (removed later the same day: "Intrinsic hydrostatic truth: one methodological line").
  `hse_crossing = 'fit-failed'` and `hse_fit_ok_barnes21_nosf_fit_mu` follow the converged flag
  `barnes21_nosf_fit_mu_ok` (test: False/`fit-failed` on a failed fit, True/`single` on a converged
  one). Spec hashes (full digests in INTERFACES section 5), superseding those above: v4
  `a2ff96a46856a952`, v4_tng300 `68f47de83d0ee6c5`, v4_tng100 `ad48c3f8d75bc256`, v4_tng50
  `d67b9bbc86ffbea8`. The target-list views are unchanged (byte-identical tables); only the
  spec hash in the sidecars and reports changes.
- **Data package** (`$TNGXMM_DATA/tables/splits/`, new files only, nothing overwritten, lines appended
  to `tables/SHA256SUMS`, `tngxmm data verify` ok): `halos_tng300.csv` `e9c89a87…`,
  `split_tng300_by_object.csv` `3845fd08…`, `halos_tng100.csv` `fbb08901…`,
  `split_tng100_by_object.csv` `51e62d3d…`, `halos_tng50.csv` `5b034f51…`,
  `split_tng50_by_object.csv` `7365d5ab…`, `halos_tng-cluster_v4_full.csv` (= `campaigns/v4/halos_v4_input.csv`,
  the 9-snapshot TNG-Cluster halo table) `579bf2ca…`. The previous `SHA256SUMS` is kept as
  `FULLSNAP/out/SHA256SUMS.before` (scratch). Release upload (`tngxmm data push`) not done.
- **Bug fixed on the way:** `truth_columns` read the converged flag of a fitted estimator from
  `<estimator>_fit_ok` while `_literature_estimators` writes `<estimator>_ok`
  (`barnes21_nosf_fit_ok`), so `hse_fit_ok_barnes21_nosf_fit` was always False and, with the
  fitted headline of 2026-10-12, `hse_crossing` was `fit-failed` for EVERY halo (the mass columns
  themselves were right). The tests had set the wrong key by hand. Fixed with a regression test on
  a computed halo; trees generated with the unfixed code carry the wrong flag
  (`hse_crossing` and `hse_fit_ok_*`; recompute from the truth-profile `summary_json`).


## Intrinsic hydrostatic truth: one methodological line (2026-10-12, author)

- **One fitted Barnes truth.** The headline is `barnes21_nosf_fit_mu`: Barnes et al. (2021) secs.
  2.1.5 and 2.4 (Vikhlinin fits, analytic slopes, 50 linear shells to 1.5 R500c); star-forming gas
  excluded, which is OUR deviation (Barnes et al. state no gas cut for the 3-D profiles), with each cell's actual thermal pressure (its own mu) instead of an assumed mu = 0.59.
  The mu = 0.59 twin `barnes21_nosf_fit` is no longer emitted under v4: no `m500_hse_`,
  `mhse_`, `mu_hse_` or `hse_fit_ok_` column, no `native/barnes21_nosf_fit` dataset in the
  truth-profile files, no `'thermal-fit'` headline variant (`variant_for` raises). The code stays
  behind `compute_hse_truth(mu059_fit=True)` (`MU059_FIT_ESTIMATOR`, default off, never written to
  the `native/` datasets or the columns) for one unit test (it differs from the headline only through
  the cells' departure of mu from 0.59). The spec value `hse_pressure = 'thermal-fit-mu'` is unchanged.
  The truth-profile format stays 3 (a dataset fewer; readers look up `native/<headline>`). Trees
  generated before this carry the extra columns and datasets; they are ignored by the readers.
- **The finite-difference non-SF estimator `barnes21_nosf` is not emitted either (addendum,
  2026-10-12, author).** It was the headline until that morning (finite-difference slopes,
  mu = 0.59) and is the column most likely to be mistaken for `barnes21_nosf_fit_mu`. Under v4 and
  the box presets: no `m500_hse_barnes21_nosf_msun`, `mhse_barnes21_nosf_r500c_over_m500c`,
  `mu_hse_barnes21_nosf` column (TRUTH_COLUMNS 70 to 67), no `native/barnes21_nosf` dataset, no
  selectable headline variant (`('thermal', 'nonsf', linear:50, 'mass')` raises in `variant_for`).
  The code stays behind `compute_hse_truth(nosf_fd=True)` (`NOSF_FD_ESTIMATOR`, default off, never
  written to the `native/` datasets or the columns) for unit tests; the binned non-SF profile the
  fitted headline needs is computed as before. The legacy v32/v32_lowz presets never emitted it
  (`truth_columns` off), so their outputs are unchanged. The spherical-stage report keys
  `barnes21_nosf_atR500c` / `_M500self` became `hse_atR500c` / `hse_M500self` (the headline's).
- **Classification of every remaining intrinsic-HSE / truth column** (table in INTERFACES section
  4.1, "Intrinsic hydrostatic truth columns"): HEADLINE `m500c_hse_msun`, `hse_headline`,
  `hse_crossing` and the headline's own column set (`*_barnes21_nosf_fit_mu`,
  `hse_fit_ok_barnes21_nosf_fit_mu`); reference `m500c_particles_msun`, `m500c_zobs_msun`,
  `r500c_zobs_kpc`; every other estimator (`barnes21`, `barnes21_xcut`,
  `barnes21_tsl`, `barnes21_mock`, `pearce20_*`, `th_*`, `thkin_vol`, the six `x_*`) and the
  temperature / anisotropy scalars are DIAGNOSTICS. Not deleted (author's call).
- **Confusion risks left in the tables (not changed):** the other finite-difference Barnes columns
  share the prefix of the headline's own copy. Use `m500c_hse_msun`.
- **Exact sentence for the paper (corrected 2026-10-13: Barnes applies no gas cut):** "The intrinsic
  hydrostatic mass of each simulated cluster follows Barnes et al. (2021): from the shell gas density
  and the mass-weighted temperature-to-mean-molecular-weight ratio in 50 linear radial
  shells out to 1.5 R500c, fitted with the Vikhlinin et al. (2006) parametrisations and
  differentiated analytically, using the thermal pressure of each gas cell as simulated (its own
  mean molecular weight, not an assumed mu = 0.59). Unlike Barnes et al., who apply no gas cut to the
  three-dimensional profiles, we exclude star-forming gas cells, whose temperature is the effective
  equation-of-state value of the TNG model rather than a physical one. M500c,HSE is the mass inside the innermost
  radius beyond 0.4 R500c at which the mean enclosed density falls to 500 times the critical
  density of the snapshot."
- Superseded by this entry: "Barnes-faithful fitted HSE estimator" (2026-10-09), "Headline
  intrinsic hydrostatic mass: Barnes et al. 2021 with fitted slopes" (2026-10-12; mu = 0.59 fit as
  headline) and the headline paragraph of "Full snapshots only" (`barnes21_nosf_fit` "stays a
  column").

## pn readout-edge rows in every band; fixed SAS calibration date (2026-10-12, author)

> Superseded 2026-11-03/04 ("Independent review, round 5", B11): the default is `instrument.pn_edge_rows = 'footprint'`;
> `'drop'` removed the real rows 14-25.

- **`instrument.pn_edge_rows = 'drop'`** (v4 and the box presets; legacy presets `keep`, products
  byte-identical): pn events within 12 rows of the readout node (RAWY <= 12) are removed in EVERY
  band, after the out-of-FOV step and before any band is cut (so the merged image and all five
  band images agree; no random draw is involved). Reason: real cleaned pn event lists have no
  event at RAWY <= 12 in any band (`#XMMEA_EP`); the mocks had dropped them in band 1 only (the
  single-pixel rule) and kept 3.6-6.9 % of the pn events of bands 2-5 there. Test: pn images lose
  2-10 % of their counts in bands 2-5, MOS images are bit-identical (e2e `test_instrument_sas_ready`).
- **Exposure maps NOT changed.** What the real PPS products do: the PPS EXPMAPs come from `eexpmap`,
  which evaluates neither PATTERN nor RAWY, so they still expose the rows that the event lists no
  longer contain (checked on the SAS-ready work: real pn exposure maps are non-zero at those rows;
  "SAS-ready products: validation"). The mock does the same, so detection and the fits see the
  same exposure-to-counts relation as with real data (a ~5 % deficit of pn counts in the outermost
  49" of the CCDs against the exposure, as in real data). Header keyword `PNEDGE`.
- **Fixed calibration date for SAS detection.** `DetectSettings.analysisdate` defaults to
  `CCF_ANALYSIS_DATE = 2026-10-09T00:00:00`, the day the Valid CCF set (550 files, manifest
  sha256 `d29ec7a2…e740d`, SASDET/INSTALL.md) was downloaded; `cifbuild` is run with it, together
  with `observationdate` of the product headers, for mocks and real observations alike, so the
  CIF no longer depends on the day of the run. `sas_detect/provenance.json` records the analysis
  date and the manifest digest. Changing it requires a new CCF download and a re-run of every
  detection. (Resolves open points 1 and 5 of "SAS-ready products: open points".)
- **Spec hashes** (136 hashed fields; the new field `instrument.pn_edge_rows` changes every preset's
  hash, legacy products unchanged): v32 `9d98ddca723c815a`, v32_lowz `38791127b5f49ad7`, v4
  `5369be806c0daac5`, v4_tng300 `08fe176a767985e4`, v4_tng100 `79607f726b636386`, v4_tng50
  `c37dd761b59777fc` (full digests: INTERFACES section 5). The hashes quoted in the "Full snapshots
  only" entry (v4 `a2ff96a46856a952`, ...) were taken before the SAS-ready products were merged and
  are superseded; the committed target lists record the hash they were built with.

## Fit-fix switches for the hydrostatic fitter (2026-10-12)

> Superseded 2026-11-03 ("No tuned factors: the last replacements"): `bg_seed = 'typical-epic'` and the
> `massfit.bg_seed` module are removed (`massfit.bg_seed` accepts `annulus` and `exposed`), and
> `fit_hydrostatic.FITFIX_LEGACY` no longer exists.

Source: closure grid v4c diagnosis (`CLOSUREV4C/REPORT.md`) and the red-versus-blue diagnosis
(`PAPERPLAN/diag`). Twelve `massfit` fields, each the legacy behaviour by default, bundled as the
fit's `fitfix` switch (`fit_hydrostatic.FITFIX_LEGACY`, `--fitfix k=v,...`, `legacy`, `legacy,k=v`):
`bg_seed` (annulus | exposed | typical-epic; v4 typical-epic, `massfit.bg_seed`: the median
QPB + sky level per image of the closure grid's background-only fits, prior width
`typical_bg_prior_dex` 0.1; a mock-calibrated table, replace it by the observation's FWC level when
at hand), `pout_logergpcm3_fixed` (v4 -37), `bg_stage1` (v4 free-unseeded), `stage2_maxloops`
(v4 10), `chain_burn` (v4 stored: the stored chain is already post-burn), `protect_core_arcsec`
(v4 10; 90 is the option B), `closure_clean_bg_zero` (v4 on), and the options `cm_prior`
(Dutton and Maccio 2014, 0.11 dex), `metallicity_prior_mode` / `metallicity_gauss` (0.3 +- 0.1,
Mernier+2017), off in every preset. Spec hashes (150 hashed fields, incl. `mock_centre` and `source_list`): v32 `25bdccf026f8d613`,
v32_lowz `952733273eed7195`, v4 `ec6bd8ad49f2f516`, v4_tng300 `bdccc9ca965dec12`, v4_tng100
`f367d2be86990390`, v4_tng50 `0356b7c8a37e84f2`. `source_list = sas` (off in every preset) masks the SAS edetect_chain point sources (EXT 0, DET_ML > 10) of a mock or a real target through one code path (`--sas-sources`). v4 also fits mocks on the X-ray peak (`mock_centre = xray-peak`, 16 arcsec-smoothed merged image, as the real fits). With every switch legacy the fit is the old fit
(`--fitfix legacy` reproduces the unmodified code of 8416326 digit for digit on the closure cell
m2_z0.5_ncc_real_s2: same M500, c200 and Z at the same seed). Measured effect and open points: `FITFIX/ff2/results.md` (login-node study).

## V4 fit configuration (author 2026-10-13)

> Superseded in part. Item 2: the core protection is 16.487 arcsec since 2026-11-03 ("Independent review of the
> replacements, round 1", A8). Item 4: `cm_prior = 'auto'`, `prior_net_counts_below` and `resolve_auto_priors` were
> removed on 2026-11-03 ("No tuned factors: the last replacements"); the default is `massfit.cm_prior = 'dutton14'`
> since 2026-11-12 ("The concentration prior").

Source: author decisions of 2026-10-13 on the fit-fix study (`FITFIX/ff2`: `cells_table.md`, `n5_table.md`,
`node2_table.md`) and the methods-ledger review (`JUSTIFY/METHODS_LEDGER.md`). The defaults below are set in the
`v4` preset and therefore in the box presets (`v4_tng300`, `v4_tng100`, `v4_tng50`); `v32` and `v32_lowz` keep the
legacy fit (`FITFIX_LEGACY`, every new field at its legacy value; the legacy quick-fit e2e passes). Spec hashes
(153 hashed fields): v32 `6c6f1ec1023bffbf`, v32_lowz `5dbc6bc291e23784`, v4 `fe0603b209e0cd17`, v4_tng300
`24b49602c40e6550`, v4_tng100 `bdde8431e89e6cd0`, v4_tng50 `9714bd9e43d59158` (INTERFACES section 5).

1. **Outer pressure free** (`pout_logergpcm3_fixed = None`; v4 no longer freezes it at -37). result.json keeps
   `pout_logergpcm3` (median) and `pout_runaway` (`P_out > -30`).
2. **Core protection 90 arcsec** (`protect_core_arcsec`, the default; v4 no longer 10).
3. **SAS source lists are the point-source mask** (`source_list = 'sas'`): `edetect_chain` list, EXT == 0,
   DET_ML > `source_det_ml_min` (10), the same code path and radius rule for mocks and real data. Locations:
   mock `<view>/sas_detect/sources.csv` (`tngxmm instrument sas-detect --view-dir`), real
   `$TNGXMM_DATA/sas_detect/<obsid>/sources.csv` (a copy of the `sources.csv` of `sas-detect --real-dir`; the CLI's
   `<obsid>/sas_detect/sources.csv` layout is accepted too). A missing list fails the target
   (`error: source masking: ... source_list = sas ...`, naming every path tried and the command); there is no
   fallback to the PPS list or the mock table. The old masks stay as `--fitfix source_list=default`. The realfit
   plan masks every row with its SAS list and flags rows without one (`no_sas_list`; none exists yet for the 638
   real observations, they have to be produced with the SAS container). The closure grid keeps its own cell
   source table (its cells are synthetic clusters without a SAS run). Added after the methods-ledger review
   (C7/D6): `source_mask_radius = 'psf'` (v4; legacy `fixed`) masks each source with the PSF-derived radius of the
   campaign Lx photometry (30" minimum, until the King wing is 0.1 of the local level, at most 150"; counts and
   local level from each fitted image), not a fixed 30".
4. **Priors only for low-signal fits** (`cm_prior = 'auto'`, `metallicity_prior_mode = 'auto'`): the Dutton and
   Maccio 2014 c-M prior (0.11 dex) and the Gaussian Z prior (0.3 +- 0.1) are active when the net source counts within
   the first-guess R500 are below `prior_net_counts_below` = **20 000** and off otherwise. Signal = counts inside
   R500 of all fit images (cameras and bands, the pn out-of-time template removed, masked pixels excluded) minus the
   background expected from the count rate of the r > 2 R500 ring (`fit_hydrostatic.net_counts_r500`; first-guess
   R500 from the Lx seed). result.json records per fit `priors_auto`, `priors_active`, `prior_cm`, `prior_z`,
   `prior_signal_net_counts`, `prior_signal_snr`, `prior_signal_threshold`; provenance holds the resolved switches.
   Justification (quick chains, the new configuration: P_out free, core 90, no priors vs both priors; ratio =
   M500 fit / true):

   | fit | net counts | no priors | priors | c200 / Z without -> with |
   |---|---|---|---|---|
   | n5 072_19640608 (z 0.39) | 235 | 0.74 | 0.47 | 1.3 / 0.44 -> 4.1 / 0.28 |
   | n5 067_14675372 (z 0.68) | 404 | 0.57 | 0.54 | 9.4 / 1.96 -> 4.3 / 0.32 |
   | closure m2_z0.5_ncc_real_s2 | 1 376 | 0.53 | 0.78 | 9.7 / 0.02 -> 4.5 / 0.28 |
   | n5 040_11109838 (z 1.40) | 3 745 | 1.08 | 0.97 | 1.8 / 1.97 -> 3.3 / 0.36 |
   | n5 050_1693341 (z 0.97) | 4 092 | 0.42 | 0.97 | 1.0 / 0.75 -> 1.8 / 0.30 |
   | n5 040_2741152 (z 1.43) | 6 111 | 0.67 | 0.81 | |
   | n5 050_13155362 (z 0.86) | 7 048 | 0.37 | 0.49 | 9.7 / 0.57 -> 6.1 / 0.30 |
   | closure m4_z0.5_cc_real_s0 | 8 652 | 0.69 | 0.76 | 4.6 / 0.58 -> 4.3 / 0.35 |
   | closure m8_z0.5_ncc_clean_s0 | 9 619 | 0.59 | 0.75 | 5.9 / 0.76 -> 4.7 / 0.33 |
   | n5 067_10404019 (z 0.56) | 10 028 | 0.68 | 0.71 | 5.6 / 0.02 -> 4.7 / 0.26 |
   | n5 059_10943721 (z 0.80) | 16 815 | 0.60 | 0.65 | 5.3 / 0.25 -> 4.5 / 0.29 |
   | closure m8_z0.5_cc_clean_s2 | 18 941 | 0.77 | 0.81 | 4.3 / 0.62 -> 4.7 / 0.22 |
   | closure m8_z0.06_ncc_clean_s1 | 275 938 | 0.90 | 0.91 | 4.3 / 0.23 -> 4.3 / 0.15 |

   Below about 20 000 counts the no-prior fits run to the edges (c200 1.0-1.3 or 9-10, Z 0 or 2) and the priors help
   move 11 of the 13 fits toward the truth (median change +0.05, largest +0.55); the two where they hurt are the two faintest (235 and 404
   counts, single quick chains, scatter of the order 0.1-0.3). The gain falls with counts (+0.05 at 17-19k, +0.01
   at 276k); the bright twin and c3b mocks of the ff2 study (140k and 550k counts) move by 3-4 % with the priors. The
   threshold is the round number above the brightest fit where the priors still gave a measurable gain (19k) and
   below the brightest tested where they are immaterial; the range 19k-140k is untested. Single seeds and quick
   chains: the sign of a given fit is not significant, the pattern is.
5. **Guarded X-ray peak as the one centring rule** (`mock_centre = 'xray-peak'` for mocks, `real_centre =
   'xray-peak'` for real data, both `find_peak_centre_guarded`). The real-fit plan reads the data-package table
   `obs_centres_guarded_v4.csv` (sha256 `eb434be3f27bb72ff90a226045152ba728f96d5c54d3ca92b391685b495c67eb`,
   `tngxmm photometry obs-centres-guarded`; theta500 from the first-pass seed masses of the previous plan, stored
   per row); `obs_seed_lx_v4.csv` was re-measured at these centres (sha256 `dc61ab10b292233c...`), and
   `campaigns/realfit_v4` regenerated. 44 of 638 targets change centre (34 `ok`, 10 `edge`; 20 targets carry the `edge` flag in
   all): `campaigns/realfit_v4/centres_changed_guarded.csv`. The new tables live in the private copy
   `V4FIT/data/tables`; they are to be copied to the shared data package by the author.

Methods-ledger corrections made with this change: `mernier2017` arXiv id 1703.01183; `barnes2021` title; Planck 2013
XX (1-b) = 0.8 is sect. 2.3 and Planck XXIV 0.58 sect. 7.1.2; `c200_dutton14` is the relation for relaxed haloes;
REFERENCE_LEDGER row 49 (Pratt 2009 has no point-source excision text) now cites Vikhlinin 2006/2009 and Jennings
and Dave 2023; Barnes et al. 2021 state no gas cut for the 3-D profiles, excluding star-forming gas is our deviation
(INTERFACES truth table, the paper sentence, `compute_hse_mass`).

## XSPEC file names must be Tcl-safe (2026-10-16)

XSPEC reads its commands as Tcl. mbproj2d's rate calculation sends `fakeit none & <rmf> & <arf> & y & foo &
<scratch dir>/mbproj2d_temp_<pid>.fak & 1.0`, so a file name containing `[...]` is executed as a Tcl command. In the
CHECKS job (1887339) the scratch directory of the real fits of ACT-CL J2011.4-5725 was named after the target,
`ACT-CL J2011.4-5725 [0042341101]` (the `[obsid]` suffix of clusters with several observations: 52 of the 638 real
targets). Those fits failed mid-way with `invalid command name "0042341101"`, after the optimiser setup.
Reproduced with the real XSPEC 12 on the login node; the same command with a clean path writes the spectrum.

- `patch_apec.xspec_safe_path` refuses `[ ] { } $ " \ ;` and white space.
- `apply_xspec_tempdir` (the scratch directory) and `fit_hydrostatic.response_for` (the RMF and ARF, which reach the same
  command) call it, so such a path fails at fit start with a message naming the characters, not inside XSPEC.
- The fitter's own fit directories (`outdir_name`) were already sanitised, and the default scratch directories (TMPDIR,
  `<workdir>/xspec_tmp`) never carry a target name. The production real-fit plan is therefore not affected; the trigger
  was a study script that put the target name in `--xspec-tmpdir` (CHECKS `build_queues.py`, now sanitised the same way).
- Verified with real XSPEC through mbproj2d: a bracketed scratch path is refused up front; the sanitised name
  `ACT-CL_J2011.4-5725__0042341101__v4` gives a count rate.
- Test: `tests/unit/test_massfit_fitprep.py::test_xspec_scratch_refuses_tcl_special_characters`. It needs a
  container-bound basetemp: runtime_env.sh sets PYTEST_ADDOPTS; elsewhere it is skipped like its neighbours.

## Real-fit reference masses: like-for-like, from the curated catalogue (2026-10-17, PLAN_V41 R0)

The claim-A comparison (`references_realfit_v4.csv`) used to be assembled from the legacy V3 tables (`validation_targets.csv`,
legacy `observational_data/*.csv`), with hand-assigned labels. Three of its "HSE" families were not hydrostatic SO masses:

- Mantz+10b is Mgas/fgas: the curated catalogue says "NOT a hydrostatic mass".
- Mantz+16 is, in the curated catalogue, a lensing mass inside a gas-mass r500.
- Mahdavi+13's hydrostatic mass is evaluated inside the LENSING r500.

The Chandra-vs-Wicker gap of ×1.6 (REVIEW_PIPELINE_2026-10-17) came largely from comparing different quantities. Also, Wicker+23
was never converted to the pipeline cosmology, while the SZ and lensing masses were (×1.03, h70 → Planck15).

Now `massfit.build_realfit_targets.reference_masses` reads only the curated catalogue `obs/catalogue/reference_masses.csv`
(the table the paper's reference appendix is built from). It keeps, per family of `REF_FAMILIES`, the paper's M500c rows of
its main table whose radius is that mass's OWN r500c:

| kind | families |
|---|---|
| SZ | PSZ2, ACT DR6 |
| hydrostatic | Wicker+23 (Lovisari+20, XMM) |
| lensing (NFW fits) | Okabe & Smith 16, Hoekstra+15, Herbonnet+20 |

- Every value is converted to Planck15 with the existing, cited converters:
  - `mass_conversion.sz_m500_to_cosmology` for SZ;
  - `m500c_to_cosmology` with kind wl/hse.
  The published value is kept in the column `m500_published_1e14`.
- Outcome against the old table: SZ is identical, lensing is within 0.1%, Wicker+23 is +3.1%.
- A double cluster listed per component (Abell 115 N/S in Hoekstra+15 and Herbonnet+20) is left out of that family. It is
  listed in the summary under `references_excluded`, never chosen.
- Not families (they stay in the catalogue for the appendix): Mantz+10b, Mantz+16, Mahdavi+13, Martino+14 (its r500
  definition is not verified), Applegate+14/WtG (mass inside a fixed 1.5 Mpc sphere).

## Blue mass at any radius, fit QC flags, multi-start optimiser (2026-10-17, PLAN_V41 F1-F3)

**Blue M(<R).**
- The fit's total mass is mbproj2d's `Phys` total: the NFW of `mass_M_logMsun`/`mass_logconc` PLUS the gas self-gravity
  (`cluster.py`: `g += computeGasAccn`). On CHECKS fits, the NFW alone is 20-33% below the reported M500, so the mass
  inside a radius cannot be recomputed from (M500, c200).
- `run_one` now keeps, for the 800 chain samples of its summary, the cumulative total and gas mass on the Phys grid:
  `mass_profile_samples.npz`, about 1.6 MB.
- `massfit.fit_qc.blue_mass_within` interpolates each sample at any R. Its 500 ρc(z) crossing reproduces the fit's M500.
- Older fits are rebuilt from their chain by `fit_qc.write_mass_profile_samples` (34 s per fit with a warm cache, which
  needs XSPEC). Check on `mock_40_13701560_v2`: the crossing median is 1.469 against the fit's 1.486 [1.20, 1.84], within
  the Monte Carlo noise of 800 different samples.

**QC flags** (`fit_qc.qc_flags`), identical for mock and real fits, each a diagnostic of the fit:
- `prior_edge_c200` / `prior_edge_Z`: the posterior median lies in the outer 2.5% of the hard prior range. c200 is flat in
  ln c over 1-10 (`fit_hydrostatic.C200_PRIOR_RANGE`, now one constant used by the prior and the flag); Z comes from the
  preset's `metallicity_free_prior`.
- the fitter's `pout_runaway`.
- the guarded-peak centre flag: the mock fit's own, or the real target's `centre_flag`.
- `bg_fallback`: some image has no on-detector background annulus beyond 2 R500 (`n_bg_fallback > 0`). This is the
  field-of-view rule, i.e. the fitter's own requirement of a measured background, with no θ500 number.
- No threshold was tuned on outcomes. Flagged fits are reported and excluded from the science samples (author decision 3).

**Multi-start optimiser** (`--fitfix starts=grid`, legacy `single`):
- The two optimiser stages run from the default start and from the 6 starts of `OPTIMISER_START_GRID`: M500 seed
  ×{0.5, 1, 2} × c200 {3, 7}, each from the same initial state.
- The highest log posterior seeds the MCMC; every start's optimum is recorded in `result.json` (`optimiser_starts`).
- No spec field yet: the J1 diagnostic decides adoption, so no preset's spec hash changes. `starts=single` runs the
  identical computation as before.

The render and build_fit_targets unit tests that "failed on main" fail only outside the massfit runtime environment
(`xspec` not on PATH). Inside it (`runtime_env.sh massfit`), all 26 pass.

## Closure grid: factorial data modes and SAS-masked fit arms (2026-10-17, PLAN_V41 F4/K5)

The CHECKS closure gap (real/clean 0.916, worst for NCC at z = 0.1) confounds two things: "no background in the data"
and "background frozen in the model". It also confounds background with AGN.

**Data modes** (`closure_grid.DATA_MODES`, the cell's `background`):
- `clean`: neither sky + QPB nor AGN;
- `real`: both;
- `bgonly`: sky + QPB, no AGN;
- `agnonly`: AGN, no sky + QPB.

`instrument_components(sky_qpb, agn)` swaps only the corresponding callables of `simulate_instrument`, and every other draw
is the same: a `real` cell regenerated in a new root is bit-identical to the CHECKS one.

**Fit arms:**
- `v4sas` masks a cell with its own SAS edetect_chain list, exactly as a campaign view; it replaces
  `CHECKS/scripts/cellfit_checks.py`.
- `v4sas_bgfree` is the same, but a background-free cell keeps a free background model.
- Both seed from `lx_1mpc`, like the campaign fits.
- `DEFAULT_FIT_ARMS = ("v4", "v32")`: a run without `--arms`, the gate and the slurm template are unchanged, and the
  v4/v32 `fit_config` records (and so their resume) are unchanged.

> Since 2026-11-03 ("Independent review of the replacements, round 1", B2) the gate arm is `v1sas`; the code now
> has `DEFAULT_FIT_ARMS = ("v1sas",)` (`validation/closure_grid.py`).

## Estimands at the observed redshift; node task queue in the package (2026-10-17, PLAN_V41 F1, K7)

**`analysis.estimands`** (author decision 1). Black, red and blue of a mock view are evaluated at z_obs:
- the 500 ρc(z_obs) crossings of the particle profile and of the red curve use `compute_hse_mass.m500_at_z`, the function
  behind the campaign's `m500c_zobs_msun`, which it reproduces exactly;
- the blue mass comes from the fit's per-sample profiles.

Estimands:
- **SO**: each curve's own crossing, for claims A and B.
- **Fixed radius**: inside the true R500c(z_obs), with the band 0.5, 0.8 and 1.2 R500c, for claim C.

Red-line quality is the truth stage's own (`hse_crossing`, fit convergence): no new threshold.

**`core.node_queue`** (`python -m tngxmm.core.runner queue run`): the CHECKS task runner moved into the package. It runs one
single-core task per core, longest first, resumably, with private rate-cache copies; a task killed at the job's end is not
a failure; bracketed names are escaped.

The full unit suite (1034 tests) passes, except `test_instrument_active_ccds.py` (3 tests). Those read stdin, a data-package
prompt, and fail identically on 60ee192.

## Pipelines `analysis` and `certify` (2026-10-17, PLAN_V41 K7)

Two extra Kedro pipelines, not stages of `__default__`, registered next to `validation`.

**`analysis`**: per-fit tables, keeping every fit and every QC flag as columns, cutting nothing.
- `mock_fits`: the estimands at z_obs.
- `real_fits`: one row per (fit, like-for-like reference).
- `closure_fits`: the closure-grid records.

**`certify`**:
- `select_certification_sample`: a seeded, stratified, test-split sample, optionally excluding earlier samples' clusters.
  It reproduces the CHECKS D1 sample exactly.
- `apply_certification_rules`: verdict JSON plus a Markdown report.

**No threshold in code.**
- `certify.rules` implements generic tests: three-way interval, precision, equivalence on paired differences, fraction,
  flag-rate ratio, consistency of two medians, and report.
- Each test uses a bootstrap over rows or over groups (clusters).
- Every number is a `params:certify.rules` entry in `conf/base/parameters/certify.yml`, naming its DESIGN section.
- A rule without data is INCOMPLETE; it never passes or fails.

**Wiring.** `cites.stage_pipeline` now treats the extra pipelines' datasets as external, as it does the stages'.
`validation.closure.arms` named a non-existent arm `v4psf`; it is now `[v4, v32]`. A Kedro run of both pipelines on the
CHECKS fits completes.

## Certification run from the package (2026-10-17, PLAN_V41 K7)

A certification run is `slurm/certify.sh`: 8 steps, each a Kedro node or a package command (sample → closure plan and
generation → real targets → staging and replays → SAS queue → fit queue → `analysis` → rules). Every number is in
`params:certify` of the Kedro environment `CERTIFY_ENV`.
- `tngxmm certify stage | queue-sas | queue-fits | real-targets | plan-closure` (`certify/cli.py`, `stage.py`,
  `queues.py`) replace the CHECKS study scripts.
- Each fit task has its own work directory, its own copy of the closure grid's settled rate cache, and the massfit
  runtime of the frozen worktree.

The Kedro environment `smoke` (`conf/smoke`) runs the same chain at toy scale:
- 2 views and their AGN-off replays;
- 2 real targets;
- 2 closure cells;
- quick chains.

`tests/unit/test_kedro_pipelines.py` checks it like base and fixtures: one preset per run, no stage restating it.

Interpreters are site variables (`TNGXMM_SITE_SIM_PY`, `TNGXMM_SITE_MASSFIT_PY`), and the production tree is
`${site:TNGXMM_SITE_PROD}/campaign_v4_full/v4`. Closure seeds are donor indices (`pick_donors`), so a fresh grid takes
new z values.

## Fit crop stays 3 R500; closure background bias carried as a measured calibration (2026-10-18, PLAN_V41 S2, DESIGN v3)

Test J1c (job 1887630, 187 of 187 fits; tngxmm_dev/J1/) repeated the closure factorial cells (real, background-only;
24 cells each) and 43 CHECKS D1 views with the fit crop at 5 and 8 R500 instead of 3, every other setting fixed.
The effects are paired medians of ln M500, bootstrapped over cells or views (95% intervals):

| sample | crop 5 - crop 3 |
|---|---|
| closure real (sky + QPB + AGN) | +0.0015 [-0.0054, +0.0092] |
| closure background only | -0.0032 [-0.0121, +0.0084] |
| TNG views, blue M(<R500c true) | -0.0086 [-0.0263, +0.0002] |
| TNG views, QC-clean | -0.0075 [-0.0263, +0.0109] |

Closure blue/true at crop 3 / 5 / 8: 0.845 / 0.854 / 0.845 (real cells).

**Decisions.**
1. The crop stays at 3 R500. It is not the cause of the closure deficit: the closure cells are equivalent within
   the ±0.015 sensitivity tolerance. The TNG-view interval reaches -0.026. By the strict rule S2 is therefore
   INCONCLUSIVE for TNG views, and the -0.9% median is recorded as the crop systematic.
2. The deficit of the real closure cells is carried as a measured calibration of the method, reported by
   (z, profile). It is about 10% low with a realistic background, against 0.97-1.03 on clean data. Attribution
   (J1 F4, J1b, J1c): it needs the sky + particle background. It is not the crop, the background's spatial shape
   (~2% match), SAS detection, the AGN light or the optimiser.
3. Gating moves to the clean cells and the catastrophic tail of the real cells (DESIGN v3 R4_clean,
   R4_real_tail). This change was made after seeing v2; it is stated in DESIGN v3 §6.
4. New generic rule test `tail` (certify.rules): the catastrophic fraction alone.
## Sensitivity tests S1, S3, S4, S5, S6: login-node results, dev presets and the SENS job (2026-10-18)

**Run now on the login node** (`internal/ledger_tests/`, results in `SENSITIVITY_TESTS.md` R7-R11; raw output in
`tngxmm_dev/SENS/`):
- S4: the production main draw reproduces exactly; gate P passes in all 100 draws of the 5 widths; only the width RATIO
  acts (`d^2` is scale invariant), the KS distances move by 0.003-0.007 (3-8 times the seed spread).
- S5: `n_nearest` is immaterial, `cap_factor` is not (+16 % / -15 % in the median donor distance at 1.5 / 3).
- S1 (b), (c): the production spectral grid and area are inside the tolerance in the band of the fit (0.23 %); 1250 and
  2500 bins are not (1.15 %); the area bound is 2525 cm^2.
- S3: the SAS lists stop at DET_ML 10, so the DET_ML 6 arm needs a new detection; S6: the largest annulus effect on the
  first-guess R500 is 6 %.

**Package changes (minimal, tested):**
- Dev presets `v4_phot1250`, `v4_phot2500`, `v4_phot10000`, `v4_phot20000`, `v4_area8000` (`config.PHOTON_SAMPLING_VARIANTS`):
  v4 with one `sim` number changed and `sim.rng_campaign = 'v4'`, like `v4_spheregas`, so a regenerated view has production's
  random streams. Their parameter files and the hash table are generated (`make params`).
- `DetectSettings.likemin / mlmin` (None = the task defaults) and the queue-entry field `detect` of `sas-queue`: a detection
  with other thresholds and `out` elsewhere, without touching the products.
- `certify.queues`: a fit configuration may carry `args` (further `fit-hydrostatic` options verbatim, e.g. `--det-ml-min 6`).

**The job** is `tngxmm_dev/SENS/slurm/sens_vera.sh` (one node; plan builder and analysis in `SENS/scripts`), made of package
commands and the node queue only. Its analysis pairs each arm with the `v4s1` fits per view and gives the median paired
change of `ln_br_1` with a bootstrap interval and the verdict against 0.015; the re-seeds `v4s2`, `v4s3` and a repeat of
`v4s1` with the code under test are the null of the statistic.

## SENS job results: photon numerics, point-source rule, seed annulus, kernel widths (2026-10-18, ledger S1, S3, S4, S6)

Job 1887659 (frozen 784f4cf; tngxmm_dev/SENS, report results/sens_report.md) ran 368 fits, 60 generations and 67 SAS
runs, all ok. Statistic: the paired median change of ln(blue/red) at 1.0 R500c against the v4s1 fits of the 43 CHECKS D1
views (QC exclusions on either side dropped), bootstrap 95% over views, tolerance ±0.015.

| arm | change [95%] | verdict |
|---|---|---|
| control (v4s1 rerun with the code under test) | +0.003 [+0.000, +0.008] | PASS |
| MCMC re-seeds v4s2 / v4s3 | +0.001 [-0.003, +0.006] / -0.000 [-0.008, +0.002] | PASS |
| DET_ML > 14 / DET_ML > 6 (re-detected, likemin 5, mlmin 6) | -0.006 [-0.012, +0.007] / -0.000 [-0.007, +0.004] | PASS |
| core protection 0 / 45 / 135 arcsec (90 baseline) | +0.013 [-0.002, +0.041] / +0.009 [-0.004, +0.024] / -0.008 [-0.022, -0.004] | INCONCLUSIVE |
| seed R500 x0.8 / x1.25 | -0.004 [-0.013, +0.009] / -0.002 [-0.018, +0.013] | PASS / INCONCLUSIVE |
| photon grid 1250 / 20000 bins (regenerated, 9 / 8 pairs) | ±0.2 intervals | INCONCLUSIVE (regeneration is a new realisation) |

Product level (S1): event band counts of the 10000- and 20000-bin arms agree with production (5000 bins) to 0.2% in total
over 12 views (PASS of the 0.5% rule). The image-level comparison fails even for the 20000-bin arm against production
(the same grid at event level), so it measures something other than the grid and is not used. The 8000 cm^2 arm changes
events by up to 7%, as expected of a different thinning draw; the 4000 cm^2 area has a margin of 1.58 over the largest
summed ARF peak, so no photon is lost (login-node S1c).

**Decisions.**
1. `sim.photon_sampling_numerics` becomes a validation: grid error 0.23% at 5000 bins (S1b), event level within 0.2% of
   20000 bins, area margin 1.58.
2. The DET_ML threshold is validated (6, 10, 14 equivalent). The 90 arcsec core protection stays open: the effect is
   bounded at a few per cent but not shown equivalent (|median| ≤ 0.013).
3. `photometry.legacy_apertures` becomes a validation through its only channel, the seed R500. The annulus moves it by
   at most 6%. Arms of -20% and +25% change the mass by ≤ 0.004 at the median; the +25% arm is INCONCLUSIVE at
   [-0.018, +0.013].
4. `method.matched_kernel_widths` becomes a validation (S4: gate P passes in all 100 draws of x0.5 and x2 width ratios;
   KS shifts ≤ 0.1 of the critical D).
5. `method.maximal_list_donors` stays open: the cap factor moves the donor distance by ±15% (S5), beyond the 10%
   tolerance. Author decision pending.

## ACT DR6 masses keep their 1/h conversion, now with its sources (2026-10-19, refactor finding C1)

The refactor's literature check found that ACT DR6 Eq. 4 has no h, so re-solving it literally for a new cosmology
gives about 0.99, where `mass_conversion.sz_m500_to_cosmology` applies about 1.03 (+3 to 3.5 %).

**Decision.** Keep the 1/h conversion and cite the chain behind it:
1. DR6 sets its normalisation to give masses consistent with the stacked weak-lensing masses of Robertson et
   al. 2024 (DR6 p. 7).
2. Robertson et al. assume H0 = 70 (their p. 2).
3. A lensing mass at fixed signal scales as 1/h.

The DR6 masses are therefore h70 lensing-equivalent masses. Ledger: `method.cosmology_conversion`, two new
evidence blocks. Citation: `robertson2024`.

## Reference-table metal axis: a bug in the photon normalisation, measured before any regeneration (2026-10-19, refactor finding A1)

The mock's photon normalisation reads the reference emission table at log10(Z / 0.023) (`load_halo_gas`;
temet's convention). The table is built as H+He plus 10^m times the APEC metals at their AG89 abundances
(`build_apec_table`), so its own unit is the AG89 metal mass fraction over H..Zn: 0.019366, computed by
`solar_metal_mass_fraction('angr')`. With 0.023, the metals are weighted x0.84.

Correcting it raises the expected photons per cell (0.5-2 keV, Z = 0.3 Z_sun):
- +1 % at kT >= 3 keV;
- +2 % at 2 keV;
- +9 to +13 % at 0.5-1 keV.

The photon energies come from the per-element spectra and are not affected.

The 0.023 was a module constant, outside the spec hash.

**Decisions.**
1. The unit becomes the spec field `sim.table_metal_unit`. None means the legacy 0.023, and the field joins
   the hash only when set, so every existing preset and product keeps its hash.
2. The dev preset `v4_zag89` uses 0.019366 with production's random streams. Paired with production views,
   it measures the effect on the masses.
3. The temet-exact reference luminosities keep 0.023: they reproduce the official TNG products.
4. Whether the production campaign is regenerated is decided on the measured mass effect.

## pn Small Window width: already the Handbook's 63 x 64 (2026-10-19, refactor finding B7)

The refactor's instrument check read the window's column range as 65.5 pixels (`PN_SW_Q_RANGE_ARCMIN`) against the
Users Handbook's 63 x 64 (Table 3). That range runs through the centres of the dead gap lanes either side of CCD 4,
and the detector mask removes the lanes, so the window images 62.99 columns x 63.99 rows of active pixels. No
change. The unit test now pins the active size to 63 x 64 within half a pixel.

## pn pattern efficiency: SAS convention verified, fixed for mocks and fits together (2026-10-19, refactor finding B3)

Verification: SAS arfgen was run on PATTERN-selected spectra of mock-header event lists (tngxmm_dev/pattern_check/).
- pn: arfgen(PATTERN<=4) = all-pattern ARF x FRAC_SD to 0.2 % at 0.5-8 keV. Our `onaxis_arf` equals arfgen's
  all-pattern ARF to 0.05 %.
- MOS: our ARF already contains the PATTERN<=12 loss through the legacy `mos_areacorr`, and agrees with arfgen
  within 5 %. No change.

Consequence: the v4 mocks (pn ratio FRAC_SD / FRAC_SDTQ = 1) and the v4 fits (the all-pattern pn ARF) agree
with each other, but both over-predict a real PPS pn image by 0.3 / 1.2 / 2.3 / 4.4 / 5.0 % at
0.5 / 1 / 2 / 5 / 8 keV. For real data the hard bands carry the temperature.

**Decision.** Mocks and real data keep ONE code path (author's constraint). There is no switch on the data's
kind.
1. The mocks record the pn PATTERN<=4 loss: `instrument.pattern_efficiency = 'sas'`.
2. Every fit uses the PATTERN<=4 pn ARF: `massfit.pn_arf = 'pattern'`, `PN_<filter>_pat4.arf` of
   `build_responses`, from the same FRAC_SD table.
3. Both fields join the hash only when set. Together with the metal-axis fix they form preset `v41` (v4
   random streams).

The production campaign changes only by regenerating with v41, which is decided with the ZAG89 result.

## Release V4.1 (2026-10-19): one coherent production configuration

> Since release 1.0.0 (2026-10-19, CHANGELOG): the presets `v41*` are named `v1*`, and
> `config.PRODUCTION_PRESET = 'v1'`.

The production release is V4.1: package version 4.1.0, tag `v4.1.0`, `config.PRODUCTION_PRESET = 'v41'`,
`config.PRODUCTION_PRESETS = (v41, v41_tng300, v41_tng100, v41_tng50)`.

**Presets.** Each release preset is its v4 preset with the October 2026 fixes that change the mocks or the fit
(`config._with_v41_fixes`):
- the reference-table metal axis in its own AG89 unit ("Reference-table metal axis");
- the pn PATTERN<=4 loss in the mocks and the PATTERN<=4 pn ARF in every fit ("pn pattern efficiency").

It keeps the random streams of its v4 preset, so every view pairs with the v4 view of the same
(snap, halo, view). Mocks and real data go through one code path: no switch on the data's kind anywhere.

**Wiring.**
- **Configuration:** `params:preset` is v41. `globals paths.campaign_prod` is
  `<prod>/campaign_v41_full/v41`; the v4 tree stays read-only at `campaign_v4_full`. `paths.responses` is
  `<MBPROJ>/responses_v41`: the production set, byte-identical, plus `PN_<filter>_pat4.arf`.
- **Certification:** the closure arm is `v41sas` and the fit tags are `v41`.
- **Real-fit plan:** it uses the release preset and its closure arm.
- **Defaults:** the stage commands default to the release preset.
- **Job scripts:** the generation and truth templates take the preset family, v41 or v4.
- **Truth:** the truth job can hard-link the spherical cutouts of the v4 generation (`REUSE_CUTOUTS_FROM`).
  The cutouts are particle indices inside each halo's sphere, and no fix changes them. A stored cutout is used
  only if its centre and radius equal the sphere the run would select.

**Tests.**
- The fixture environment keeps `preset: v4`: its golden products are v4 physics.
- The release preset has its own e2e test: Kedro equals `campaign run` on the fixture halo, and the pn images
  carry PATEFF < 1.

**Order of the production work.**
1. A one-node pilot: the showcase chain with v41 on three views, and the generation template on a few
   snapshots.
2. The regeneration with v41.
3. The truth stage with the reused cutouts.
4. The confirmation run under DESIGN v3.1.
5. The population fits, only if its gate opens.

## Realism, release 1.1 (2026-10-24)

One section for everything that release 1.1 changes to make the mock images indistinguishable from the real ones, in place
of the ten sections of the realism work (donor background and live time, anomalous MOS CCDs, residual soft protons, corner
QPB, the in-FOV background as three components, cutout gas, uniform gas sphere, box cutouts, convergence test; `git log
-- docs/dev/DECISIONS.md` keeps them). What stands is below; what was tried and rejected is listed last and is not in the code.

### Geometry of the mock gas (author's decisions of 2026-10-22 and 2026-10-24)

* **Cube cutouts.** `sim.loader = 'cutout'` with `sim.mock_cutout_shape = 'box'`: for every halo of every simulation the stored
  gas (PartType0) is ALL the gas inside the axis-aligned cube of half-side `sim.mock_box_r500` = 4 R500c about GroupPos,
  from `<tree>/mock_cutouts/<sim>/snap_<snap>.h5` (format `tngxmm-mock-cutouts-1`, delta-encoded sha256-checked snapshot indices,
  gas only; `campaign mock-cutout`, Kedro node `sim.cut_mock_gas`, `slurm/mock_cutout.sh`, resumable). 4 R500c is the smallest round
  multiple whose cylinder contains the 1.5 Mpc photometric aperture of the smallest halo (R500c 0.41 Mpc, so 1.5 / 0.41 = 3.66;
  4 R500c = 1.64 Mpc) and fits every stored truth sphere (r_cut >= 4.24 R500c). The hydrostatic truth stays spherical on its own
  `truth_cutouts`. The stored spheres of the cutout loader without `mock_cutout_shape` remain an alternative mode.
* **The emitting volume is the inscribed CYLINDER (new).** `sim.mock_gas_region = 'cylinder'` (the production value; 'cube' = the whole
  cube, the cylinder that encloses it with radius sqrt(2) x half-side): the photons come from the cylinder of radius and
  line-of-sight half-depth 4 R500c taken along each view's axis (views are axis-aligned, `generate_photons.LOS_AXIS_IDX`).
  Why: a circular edge instead of a square imprint, the same +-4 R500c depth at every projected radius below 4 R500c, and the
  1.5 Mpc aperture covered for every halo. The photon filter of `generate_photons` does it (`photon_switches`); the gas outside
  the cylinder is in the file but never emits. A spec field hashed at its production value, so every v1.0 spec hash changed.
* **Coarse gas is kept** (author's decision): cells heavier than 8 x the median cell mass of the cutout (the low-resolution gas
  of a TNG-Cluster zoom; an empirical rule, not a reference) emit like any cell, are counted and reported with their mass and
  0.5-2 keV share (`gas_cutout` of `obs_meta.json`). Nearest low-resolution dark matter of the 2463 TNG-Cluster targets: median 6.1 R500c
  (5/25/75/95 per cent 2.9 / 4.7 / 7.8 / 11.4); inside 4 R500c for 15 per cent. A view whose cube holds it can be flagged
  from the file; the 7.2 R500c fixture halo has it at 6.78 R500c.
* **Convergence** (`tngxmm.sim.outskirts_convergence run --shape cube`, 20 TNG-Cluster halos, three views, rule stated before the run: the
  emission outside H R500c is < 1 per cent of the annulus in 0-1 R500c AND < 1 per cent of the median XMM background in all eight
  annuli, at the 90th percentile). Inside 4 R500c projected the result is the same for the cylinder as for the cube: the core criterion
  passes (< 0.12 per cent) and the background criterion passes at P90 <= 0.62 per cent within the footprint. The literal rule fails
  only beyond the footprint (1.5-2.6 per cent of the background at P90), from structure beyond about 6 R500c that no stored cutout holds.
* **60-view box realism** (the views of `INDIST2/jobs60_out.csv` regenerated with the cube gas and replayed count-matched): the
  large-field background deficit is -0.046 dex, equivalent within noise to the sphere and the original-zoom variants of the same views,
  so the geometry is not what is left of the deficit.

### Instrument stage (production values, hashed `InstrumentSpec` fields)

Each switch below has a ledger entry (`docs/assumptions/assumptions.yml`, `tngxmm ledger check`: 104 entries, 3 open).
`detector_footprint = 'donor-pps'` (the donor's own exposure maps, not one observation's mask); `vig_energy_rule = 'band-centre'` (SAS eexpmap);
`vignetting_beyond = 'ccf-edge'`; `vig_axis = 'ccf'` (the telescope axis of each camera from XMM_MISCDATA OPTICS_X/Y/CCD,
`instrument.calibration_axis`: it agrees with the donor-measured centres to 0.05 arcmin for the pn and 0.16 for the MOS) and
`mos_rga_vignetting = 'pps-measured'` (a dipole 0.0125 per arcmin about the vignetting centre; no calibration file carries an
azimuthal term, so this one stays measured on the PPS maps and is marked open in the ledger); `live_time = 'donor-expmap'`;
`background = 'donor'` (flat particle level per PPS band of the donor, beyond 1.5 R500); `qpb_spatial = 'fwc'`; agn_lognlogs
`moretti03-soft`, `agn_psf = 'ellbeta'`, `agn_galactic_nh = 'view'`, `agn_norm = 'moretti03'`; `galactic_nh = 'willingale13'` (N_HI + 2 N_H2;
the fit keeps HI4PI, as the real fits do); `mos_soft_ccd = 'donor'` (only MOS1-4, MOS1-5, MOS2-2, MOS2-5, the CCDs of the anomalous state of
Kuntz & Snowden 2008).

**Background model.** MOS: three measured components per donor (`qpb_level = 'corners-band'`, `soft_protons = 'inout'`): the QPB from the
out-of-FOV corners band by band within the PPS flare GTIs, the residual soft protons from the De Luca & Molendi IN/OUT 10-12 keV excess
with the CXB subtracted, and the vignetted sky; the sum reproduces each donor's 4.5-12 keV counts to 1.5-2 per cent. pn: keeps the
in-FOV factors, because a pn soft component is unmeasured: the sum check over-predicts the pn 4.5-12 keV counts by 25 per cent and the pn
1-4.5 keV background falls 0.13-0.16 dex short. The corner QPB alone (without the soft-proton component) made the replicas LESS like
their donors (statistics C2ST 0.732 / 0.794 -> 0.950 / 0.954), so the three components are one switch group. On the 195 development
views with `agn_norm moretti03` the large-field background deficit fell from -0.079 to -0.030 dex, statistics C2ST -0.029 +- 0.015.

**Tried and rejected** (code, tables and tests removed): the donor-conditioned AGN normalisation (`donor-pps`, `donor-pps-relative`; the
audit rejected the relative norm), the donor soft-proton population (`soft_protons = 'donor'`; the images cannot separate it from the QPB), the
PPS-radial MOS vignetting and the single-scale corner QPB.

### Donor tables (pipeline `donor_tables`, data package `tables/`, sha256 in `SHA256SUMS`)

Built by nodes, not scripts: `build_donor_nh_total`, `build_donor_background` (in-FOV flat level, nominal-QPB scale and live time per camera and
band), `build_donor_soft_ccd`, `build_donor_conditions`, and for the MOS corner QPB and IN/OUT rates `fetch_pps_archive`,
`build_fwc_reference`, `measure_mos_background`, `build_mos_background` (the measure node is shardable and writes one JSON per donor to
`paths.donor_tables_work`; the build node assembles, so a sharded run never writes a partial package table). Coverage of the 638 donors:
nh_total 638; background 637; soft_ccd 635 donors (20,483 rows); mos_background: event lists 637 of 638 (0720700101 has no PPS event
list), flare series FBKTSR 638, MOS1 633 and MOS2 635 corner/IN-OUT rows, six donors keep the in-FOV factors for one or both cameras (0502671801,
0692933101, 0720700101, 0827010101, 0827031301, 0894000401); the IN/OUT excess is finite for 517 / 516 donors, the rest lack a PPS image or
exposure map in the IN region and get no soft-proton component (NaN in the table, which the consumer reads as none: the old script wrote 0).
Reproduction of the development tables: background 5e-10 (the QPB band columns 3-4e-4, from the old script's four-digit nominal rates),
soft_ccd 5e-10 with all 244 classification flags equal, MOS corner table 2.5e-16, FWC reference 1.4e-16, conditions on every common column.

* A donor without a PPS MOS image (0502671801, 0720700101, 0827010101) has no anomalous-CCD measurement: `donor_soft_ccd` gives it an empty
  map flagged `unmeasured` (header `SOFTCCD = unmeasured`), and raises `KeyError` only for a donor whose MOS images exist but whose entry
  is missing.
* `donor_conditions` has 593 rows (the donors of the replica targets), 577 of them in the candidate POOL (a background row with finite live
